## 合并档位计数/可配置ICG及流水BF16-FP32数字校准RTL / calibration-v2-193-rtl-20261027

**RTL_FUNCTIONAL / functional_only_eda_prepared**。N64 M1/M8 B4T64/B7T128 exact aligned single reduction tree and temporal sum; optional 6/3 pipeline cuts and real-ICG interface. Digital FP32/BF16 add/product/conversion/nonfused MAC, 8 lanes, one/16 accumulation contexts. No PRNG/SRAM/CIM/CTS.

条件：`{"simulator": "Icarus Verilog 10.1", "functional_period_ns": 10, "target_node_nm": 55, "target_frequency_mhz": [100, 250, 500], "technology_bound": false, "real_icg_cell_unknown": true}`。

## FFN第一轮精确计数与整数恢复校准RTL / ffn-stage1-193-rtl-20261025

**RTL_FUNCTIONAL / functional_only_eda_prepared**。N64; M1/M8; B4/B7; T64/T128; W7mag+sign; exact count CW20/21; integer restore coeff16/rshift6; signed64 saturating local sum. No OR, PRNG, comparator, SRAM, nonlinear or BF16 conversion.

条件：`{"simulator": "Icarus Verilog 10.1", "functional_clock_period_ns": 10, "target_node_nm": 55, "target_frequency_MHz": 100, "technology_bound": false, "seeds_M1": [17, 31, 193], "seeds_M8": [17]}`。

## 成熟FPnew BF16/FP32单元及折叠DOT4 N64前布局PPA / matrix-bf16-204-20261028

**DC_GATE_PT_PRELAYOUT / calculated_with_limits**。Specialized FPnew BF16xBF16/FP32; non-fused folded DOT4 full N64 includes four FP32 contexts and final reduction. 限制：Prelayout; no CTS/SDF/SPEF or silicon signoff. Original timing failures and power warnings retained. Supplemental STA reset contract is an external requirement, not a completed reset-distribution circuit.；FPnew arithmetic is mature upstream; folded four-lane DOT4 is this task conventional non-fused integration, not a proven globally optimal BF16 matrix engine. FP32 rounding at every add differs from fused DOT instructions.；Both scalar and complete N64 windows include gross internal/switching/leakage. No repeated64x scalar proxy for frame energy. Data/weight supply, SRAM, RNG and front/backends remain excluded.；Synthetic three seeds only. BF16 versus OR4/T32 SC does not establish matched quality or full-array advantage. Prior INT8 runs use different traces/boundaries.；V1 old Python; V2 timescale; V3 success marker; V4/V5 DC2018 compatibility failures preserved. No arithmetic algorithm edits. Generator regeneration requires reapplying recorded equivalent compatibility patches.；Frozen driver operation_definition says scalar for all tops. For dot64_4 each operation is one complete N64 dot product, 64 products; windows contain16 frames over300ns each, i.e.4800ns per profile. Power/time/operations numeric fields are correct.；The original shared reset constraints assume deassertion0.02..1ns from rising edge. FPnew async reset removal violates this. Separate STA on identical mapped bytes uses explicit reset release4.5..5.5ns, matching the falling-edge bench. No recovery/removal false path, data constraint change or power recomputation.

下一步：Review PPA and timing/reset contract; compare precision-matched INT8 with classical output-stationary MAC / balanced reduction and shared data delivery; fused BF16 dot and clock gating remain possible optimizations.

## 参数化popcount/移位加法/饱和累计及组合能耗表征 / matrix-modules-204-20261028

**DC_GATE_PT_PRELAYOUT / calculated_with_limits**。33 fixed tops: popcount8..64 with output LSB quantization, signed popcount difference, mask/OR4, fixed shift-add, signed7..11 saturation counters, two planes, three77/54/50 state-bit banks, variable alignment16/20/24. No artificial I/O flops on combinational tops. SRAM/RNG/encoding/physical broadcast/CTS/SDF/SPEF excluded; CE not ICG.

条件：`{"node_nm": 55, "corner": "SS", "voltage_V": 1.08, "temperature_C": 125, "clock_MHz": 100, "seeds": [17, 503, 1009], "window_ns": null, "load_pF": 0.02, "sdf": false, "spef": false, "cts": false, "activity": "Three seeds and sparse/dense/replay/idle per top. Synthetic stress and derived old M8 replay; non16 pop replay IIDp=.35, align replay random words. Sequential32 samples/frame; fixed shift replay once/35cycles. No model quality claim.", "area_reporting": "raw library area and user-confirmed raw*0.81 55nm-equivalent area both preserved; do not scale power/timing"}`。
